# Agents · R · part 20

1,109 entries, reviewer to reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **reviewer**: Independent code review, separate from whoever built it — correctness first, then simplification. Use after every builder task on Standard/Complex Tier projects, and for the whole-product YAGNI trim before Publish. ([GRU953/GRU953-Studio](https://github.com/GRU953/GRU953-Studio/blob/HEAD/plugins/gru953-studio/agents/reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **reviewer**: Read-only, provider-neutral review of scope, diffs, contracts, and verification evidence. ([Grzanka777/NeuralEngine](https://github.com/Grzanka777/NeuralEngine/blob/HEAD/.qwen/agents/reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **reviewer**: Code review. Reviews the current diff (or a named set of files) for bugs, security issues, and IaC misconfiguration and reports only real findings with severity and file:line. Read-only; it never edits files or runs anything that changes state. For judgment calls and stuck loops, use the advisor agent. ([gtlpanda/PandaStack](https://github.com/gtlpanda/PandaStack/blob/HEAD/graphs/qa/agents/reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **reviewer**: This agent reviews contracts and proposals against your standard positions and playbook. It checks for missing clauses, contradictions, and deviation from your norms. ([guntherb7/helm](https://github.com/guntherb7/helm/blob/HEAD/business/.claude/agents/reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **reviewer**: The squad's reviewer. Automated PR reviewer triggered by CI (GitHub Action) on every opened or updated pull request. Detects which subproject(s) the diff touches, applies that subproject's required idioms plus the constitution's non-negotiables, finds programmatic bugs and violated patterns, and posts a structured, machine-readable review on the PR via the GitHub API (gh). Stack-agnostic: reads the project profile at runtime. ([gustavobaptista/specops](https://github.com/gustavobaptista/specops/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md) · ★1)

- **reviewer**: Use this agent when .trio/handoff.md exists and the builder's work needs evaluation. Part 1이 FAIL이어도 Part 2를 수행해 재구현 라운드에 품질 지적을 함께 전달한다. oh-my-gx:gx-tdd phase-review(전체 브랜치)와 phase-implement Step 2-V(태스크 범위 모드) 전용 — 구 spec-reviewer/quality-reviewer 2석을 대체한다. &lt;example&gt; Context: phase-review Step 2 진입 user: (오케스트레이터) diff가 PRD의 AC를 충족하는지, 코드 품질이 적절한지 한 번에 리뷰해줘 assistant: reviewer가 Part 1에서 AC 매트릭스(AC-1 ✅, AC-2 ❌)와 spec_verdict FAIL을 확정한 뒤, Part 2에서 Critical 0·Important 2건([동작불변])과 quality_verdict를 보고 — AC-2 관련 지적은 "재구현 대상"으로 표기 &lt;/example&gt; &lt;… ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
